One-time `TechErrorCountsInFiguresMigration` (KEY `2026-10-17-tech-error-counts-in-figures` — never rename after release; waits for the SLA-tiles marker (chain calc → embed → SLA → counts); 10 min after boot (the rollout window — see the rollback note), hourly, `scheduler_lock` `tech-error-counts-in-figures-migration`, ≤ 24 attempts per pod, marker re-read inside the lock and written only after ALL channels; `saveTemplate(…, null, "SiteMonitor")` conditional, ≤ 3 retries): a HOSTED `unmapped` / `quota` section that is EXACTLY the 4a default (title TR / EN, count `number` + note `textarea` with the default labels, no hint / link / calc / target / direction; any host id) is dissolved into the `summary` metrics section after `error-count` (map first, then quota, end of the list when `error-count` is gone) and the section is removed; skipped when there is no SEPARATE (un-hosted) `summary` metrics section, the section was changed, the team un-hosted it, or the 30-field cap would be exceeded; values survive by id (`carry` on "apply latest template": `lost_values` 0, rates recomputed). Test oracle: `TechErrorTestTemplates.hostedDefaults(lang)` = the 4a / 4b layout (written titles — a real oracle for the old-shape match); the 4a embed and 4b tiles migrations now produce THAT layout and the chain `… → TechErrorCountsInFiguresMigration.migrate` must reproduce today's default byte-identically (`TechErrorSummaryEmbedMigrationTest.bothShapesBecomeToday`, `TechErrorSlaTilesMigrationTest.before4bBecomesToday` derive `before4b` from the hosted layout); `TechErrorTemplateTest.hostStrictAndLenient` and `TechErrorReportPdfWriterTest.summaryEmbeddedNotesAndFacts` run on it (the host feature stays tested). **Blank notes (review 2026-10-17):** a BLANK `textarea` of a metrics section is NOT printed in the PDF or the mail (`TechErrorReportPdfWriter.blankText`; an all-blank-note section prints no title) — the same rule the hosted notes had; a blank short `text` field keeps "—". On screen the generic metrics layout still draws the two notes AFTER the compact fields (grid, then long texts), not literally after `error-count`. Rollback note: a pre-release-7 pod shows the same template (plain metrics fields) — nothing is lost; the migrated layout stays; a report created on an old pod during the rollout copies the hosted layout and takes the new one with "apply latest template"; a CHANNEL created on an old pod AFTER the marker was written keeps the hosted layout (one pass, marker inside the lock) — "varsayılana dön" / un-embed in the template editor. Gates: `TechErrorCountsInFiguresMigrationTest` (pure rules, waits for the SLA marker, H2 job + refresh keeps values), `TechErrorTemplateTest.defaultLayoutMatchesTheRequest`, `TechErrorReportControllerTest` (8 default sections), `TechErrorValuesTest.lenientStored` / `TechErrorReportServiceTest.refreshTemplate` (field removed from Verileri, not a section), `TechErrorReportPdfWriterTest.blankTextareaNotPrinted`.
    - **Section card look (2026-10-17, release 11, user feedback: "şablonlarda bölümleri açtığında etrafını çevreleyen çizgi kötü görüntü veriyor"):** the ugly line was the browser's DEFAULT `<fieldset>` frame (2 px groove + padding + margin) of the two edit-lock fieldsets in `TemplateEditor` — they carry `m-0 border-0 p-0` now (e2e measures every fieldset in `te-template-editor`: border / padding / margin 0). `CollapsibleCard` (report AND template editor) is ONE calm frame — `rounded-xl border-border bg-card`, no shadow, `py-0`; the toggle stays INSIDE the `h3` (WAI) but spans the full header row (`w-full`, chevron first, text wraps, ghost hover / focus ring frame the row, `pointer-coarse:min-h-10`); the copy icon stays a SIBLING of the `h3` inside `te-copy-row` at the row's END next to `headerEnd` (move / delete), both in a 42 px box (`SIDE`) aligned with the FIRST line of a wrapped title; open = one divider (`border-t` on the content, `px-4`), hint / invalid badge indented to the title text (`ps-10`); closed = a title + badge row. The header (`HEADER`) is `items-stretch` — shadcn `CardHeader`'s `items-start` in a flex column shrank the row to its content (review 2026-10-17: on a short title the toggle / hover / move-delete buttons sat next to the text). Template items (`TPL_ITEM`: fields / slots / lines / tiles / columns / rows) KEEP the 2026-10-16 "sharp" look (`border-foreground/25` + `shadow-sm`, strip `bg-muted`) — only the strip's inner radius is `calc(var(--radius-lg) - 1px)`; tokens only (no `dark:`), 12 px rhythm, ≥ 40 px targets. For SECTION titles the copy icon sits at the row's end (field labels / image names / chart captions keep it right after the text). Gates unchanged (same `data-slot`s): `SectionCollapse.test.jsx`, `TechErrorTemplateCollapse.test.jsx`, `TechErrorReportAutosave.test.jsx`, `TechErrorCopyLabels.test.jsx`; `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ends the template and report tests with all sections open (clipped / smallTargets / fieldset frame) + full-page `te-sections-template|report-<vp>.png`.

Filters are component state (no URL keys). Review round (2026-10-17): Refresh keeps a DIRTY visibility draft (`settingsRef` + `isDirty`) and is disabled while saving; a save bumps the load sequence (a GET started before the PUT can't show the old selection as saved); after a save the rows' `team_visible` follow the saved ids locally (`withVisibility`); a read-only viewer gets no link into a channel of a team the module is hidden from (`tes-open-hidden`); opening a channel with a dirty draft asks first (`te.set.leave*`); without the team directory the picker names come from the channel rows (`teamsFromChannels`); `TES_TABLE_MIN_PX` 520.     - **Channel order + period summary + team badge (2026-10-17/18, user-approved B7 / B8 + user report "takım bilgisinin yanında 2 ikon var; takıma tıklanmıyor"):** sort option `order` in `channelListModel.CHANNEL_SORTS` = team A→Z (Turkish collation, `team_id` tie so a team stays contiguous) → `sort_order` ascending (null last, 0 valid) → name; the other sorts are byte-identical. In that sort the row / card `KebabMenu` carries "Yukarı taşı" / "Aşağı taşı" (`KebabMenu` items accept `disabled` + `ariaLabel`; accessible name "Yukarı taşı: <kanal>") ONLY for `can_edit ∧ ¬shared_access`, disabled at the team's first / last position, while busy, and while a search or status / KPI filter is active (`te.sort.orderLocked` — the neighbour could be hidden; the team filter is server-side and does not disable it); the hint `te-sort-hint` shows only while a visible row is movable. A move sends the team's FULL id order in ONE request: `PUT /api/tech-error-reports/teams/{teamId}/channel-order {ids}` (gate = the channel PUT's own-scope rule `requireWrite` + `canWriteOwn` — a grant → 403, AUDIT → 403; unreadable team → 404 `TEAM_NOT_FOUND`; missing / extra / foreign / duplicate ids → 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED` `ids`); `TechErrorReportService.reorderChannels` writes 1…n in one transaction with a targeted `updateSortOrder` only where the value changes, re-reads the team's orders (a concurrent write leaving a mixed order → 409 `CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION`, rolled back) and writes ONE audit `TECH_ERROR_CHANNEL_REORDER` (`team_id`, `before` / `after` comma-joined ids — AuditDiff truncates arrays at 20) only when something changed. The client falls back to per-channel PUTs (`reorderPlan`: a swap only when every channel of the team has a filled, distinct `sort_order`, otherwise 1…n renumbering) only on 405 or a 404 without `TEAM_NOT_FOUND` (old pod during a rollout); a fallback failure after ≥ 1 saved PUT says `te.order.partial` and shows the server's order. A move bumps the load sequence (late loads dropped), stays busy until its reload settles (owner-keyed busy flags — toggle / delete / start-report never clear it; Refresh disabled), announces the result in `te-order-status` (`aria-live`) and the pager follows the moved row. A new channel gets max(team) + 1 (1 in an empty team; null while the team still has unordered channels). All `TECH_ERROR_*` audit events are in `auditFormat` SPECIAL now (they showed raw names). Kişisel Panom colour panel `stat_channel_reports_missing` (`statDefs`, `preset: false` — presets / default layout unchanged) needs `tech_error_reports.read` + the pseudo-permission `@channel_reports` (`scope.channel_reports !== false`, the nav rule) and reads `GET /api/tech-error-reports/period-summary` → `{period:{year,month}, active, sent, open, missing, inactive}` (same read scope / module visibility / 403 as the overview, same meaning as `channelListModel.channelCounts` via `TechErrorReportService.reportStatus`, no template parsing, no `reconcileQueued`, memoized per scope key for `site.monitor.tech-error-report.summary-cache-ms` 30000, ≤ 500 keys; old pod 404 / 405 → the overview); value = active channels whose period report is missing, 0 green / > 0 amber (a task, not an outage), 403 → grey "no access", no list / no active channel → grey, click → `channelreports` `te_st=missing`. **Team badge:** the channel row / card, channel header, report header and Settings rows draw ONE icon (TeamBadge's own; the extra `Users` icon is gone) and the badge opens the team members window — except on rows / headers / reports seen only through an access grant (`shared_access` → static: the members dialog would show the owner team's people); on touch the name and the team line are 12 px apart (`pointer-coarse:gap-3`, shared `ChannelNameAndTeam`) so the badge's `::after` hit area never overlaps the name button; the Settings row badge widens its touch area (`pointer-coarse:after:-inset-y-3`); the e2e touch helpers count an absolutely positioned `::after` and `badgeHitOverlaps` checks the overlap. Gates: `TechErrorReportControllerTest.channelOrder|channelOrderScope|periodSummary`, `TechErrorReportServiceTest.newChannelGetsMaxPlusOne|reorderChannelsWritesOnlyChangedRows|periodSummaryMatchesChannelCounts`, `channelListModel.test.js`, `TechErrorChannelsList.test.jsx`, `TechErrorSharedAccess.test.jsx`, `statModel.test.js`, `MyDashStatWidgets.test.jsx`, `auditFormat.test.js`, `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ("liste yeniden tasarımı": one request, 60 ids).
    - **PDF running header, table of contents, bookmarks + template preview PDF (2026-10-17/18, user-approved C15 / D18 + review):** every page except the cover carries a running header ("<kanal> · <ay yıl>" in the template language left; the resolved report title right when it fits — a fitting title of any length is printed, "too short, omit" applies only when it had to be shortened) stamped with the page numbers at the end (`PdfCanvas.setRunningHeader`; continuation content starts `HEADER_BAND` 10 pt lower; other PDFs never call it and stay byte-identical). The PDF is rendered in TWO passes (`TechErrorReportPdfWriter.write`): pass 1 is layout only (`layoutOnly`, no image embedding — layout depends on image size, never bytes); when the natural layout has ≥ `TOC_MIN_PAGES` (4) pages, pass 2 reserves the "İçindekiler" / "Contents" block on PAGE 1 under the cover band (and the sample banner) and content starts on a fresh page 2 (entries that don't fit continue at the top of page 2, content then on page 3); numbers, dot leaders and invisible links are filled AFTER the content through `PdfCanvas.drawOn(page)` from pass 2's own section marks (matched by title in order) — no page is ever inserted, so the TOC never splits a section and its numbers are the final pagination. Only PRINTED sections are listed (≤ 2 lines, then "…"). Every `EMBED_SCALES` step renders both passes. Bookmarks (`PdfCanvas.outline`) for every printed section at any length; document title = the resolved report title (`DisplayDocTitle`). Template preview: `POST /api/tech-error-reports/channels/{id}/template/preview-pdf {template}` (gate = reading the template, AUDIT and grant-only readers included; strict validation → 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED` `template`; shared PDF permits → 429; at most 6 per user per minute per pod, counted after validation → 429 `RATE_LIMITED` + `Retry-After` + `retry_after_seconds`; nothing written, inline + `no-store`, `<slug>-sablon-onizleme.pdf` / `-template-preview.pdf`) renders `TechErrorSampleValues.previewInput` (pure, deterministic: counts from the id hash, denominator-only roots 1.0–1.5 M, ratio numerators ≤ denominator / 4, `minus` ≤ numerator / 2 in ≤ 40 shrinking passes, computed fields via `TechErrorCalc.apply` and any percent / yes-no still empty (broken / manual formula) gets a sample value, last month 2–8 % lower for `{d:}` / tile deltas, every yes/no "Yes" so nothing is exempt, no images, empty trend, `Provenance` null, preparedBy = session `displayName`) with `TechErrorPdfInput.sample = true` → marked on EVERY page (page-1 banner "ÖRNEK ÖNİZLEME — …", running header "ÖRNEK ÖNİZLEME · …" / "SAMPLE PREVIEW · …", cover "Hazırlayan: <name> (örnek önizleme)", document title "… — örnek önizleme"). UI: template toolbar "Örnek PDF" (`te-tpl-preview-pdf`) sends the CURRENT draft (unsaved edits), opens the tab on click (blocked → download + `te.tpl.previewPdfDownloaded`); only an EDITOR is stopped by client-side field errors (`te.tpl.previewFixErrors`), a read-only viewer always sends and sees the server's message; the blob URL is released after `PREVIEW_URL_TTL_MS` (10 min) or when the editor unmounts. Tests that look for a title's page skip the contents page. Gates: `TechErrorReportPdfWriterTest.runningHeaderOnContinuationPages|tableOfContentsAndBookmarks|tocNeverSplitsASection|shortReportHasBookmarksButNoToc|tableOfContentsEnglish|shortTitleShowsInHeader|documentTitle|sampleBannerOnFirstPage`, `TechErrorSampleValuesTest`, `TechErrorReportControllerTest.templatePreviewPdf|templatePreviewPdfRateLimited`, `WeeklyOutagePdfWriterTest` / `ExecutiveSummaryPdfWriterTest` (shared `PdfCanvas`), `TechErrorTemplatePreviewPdf.test.jsx`, `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` (template test: preview step).

status: missing → open → sent → inactive; team), `channelCounts`; report list `reportStatusKey` (queued | sending → queued), `filterReports` (title + period label, status, year), `reportYears`, `reportCounts`; URL keys `te_q` / `te_st` / `te_sort` / `te_page` / `te_ps` (channels) and `te_rq` / `te_rst` / `te_ry` / `te_rpage` / `te_rps` (reports; `REPORT_LIST_KEYS`). `ChannelsView`: `MonitorStatsBar` period cards are the status filter (`data-slot="stat-item"` + `data-key`, `aria-pressed`; clicking the active card or "Aktif kanal" clears), toolbar card (search `data-page-search` `te-search`, team `SearchableSelect`, sort `NativeSelect` `te-sort`, `te-clear-filters`, count line `te-count` + `te-period`), list by CONTAINER width (`CHANNEL_TABLE_MIN_PX` 960: `table-fixed` rows `te-channel-row` with the period badge + the period-report button in one column and "Şablonu aç" + `KebabMenu variant="labeled"` (Tüm raporlar / Düzenle / Pasife al / Sil) in the actions column; else cards `te-channel-card` — the old card with an icon kebab; `te-channel-list` carries `data-layout`), `usePagination` preset `page` (50) + `PaginationBar`; filters live in the URL and are KEPT when drilling into a channel and back. `ChannelView`: header card with `te-channel-stats` ("N rapor · M gönderildi") + approval chip; reports toolbar (`te-rsearch`, `te-rstatus`, `te-ryear` only with ≥ 2 years, `te-rclear`, `te-rcount`), `usePagination` preset `panel` (25) over the FILTERED flat list, year headings per page (`groupByYear(pageItems)`); table ≥ 760 px else cards unchanged. `TechErrorReportsPage` deletes the report-list keys from the URL when the view is the channels list (a stale search must not follow the user into another channel) and never touches the channel-list keys. Rules kept: jsdom width 0 = table (vitest), the old tests find "Şablonu aç" per row; a blank textarea / inactive channel never counts as a problem; unknown URL values fall back to `all` / `name`. Review round 1 (2026-10-17): the year heading counts the whole FILTERED year, not the slice on the page (`yearTotals`); the pagination bar sits AFTER the year groups (`revealListTop` scrolls to the list) and renders only when there is something to page; the "Aktif kanal" card is the ACTIVE one while no filter is set (`activeFilter={status}`, same convention as ActivityLog / PushLogView — clicking the active card clears); the table kebab is labelled "İşlemler" (`te.col.actions`, accessible name "<channel> — İşlemler") in a `w-72` actions column so "Şablonu aç" + menu fit one line; search inputs carry `pointer-coarse:h-10` on the INNER `InputGroupInput` (the shadcn Input has its own `h-9`); the page container calls `flushUrlQuerySync()` before every view change (a search typed < 300 ms before opening a channel used to be lost on the way back); one live region per toolbar (the bar announces the range). **New-channel dialog (same day, user request: "şablon seçimi radyo butonlarının tasarımı proje geneline uymuyor"):** the template source (`tr` / `en` / `copy`) is a `RadioGroup` of shadcn choice cards (`FieldLabel` + `Field orientation="horizontal"` + icon / title / description — the pattern of `TeamAccessGrantDialog`; `data-slot="te-source"` / `te-source-options` / `te-source-copy`, `data-source`; the copy card spans both columns and reveals the channel picker); hints `te.source.trHint|enHint|copyHint`. Gates: `channelListModel.test.js`, `TechErrorChannelsList.test.jsx` (60 channels: 50 + 10, KPI filter, search fold, sort, deep link, period-report start keeps filters), `TechErrorChannelsList.mobile.test.jsx` (width hook 400 → cards, kebab with delete, page 2), `TechErrorReportsList.test.jsx` (30 reports: 25 + 5, status / year / search, back clears report keys only, delete confirm), `TechErrorReports.test.jsx` unchanged, `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ("liste yeniden tasarımı", 390/768/1280: KPI filter, search, sort, page 2, report filters + paging, overflow / clipping / touch targets).
    - **E-mail preview (2026-10-17, release 10, user request: "mail göndermeden önce overview edebilmeliyiz"):** `GET /api/tech-error-reports/reports/{id}/mail-preview` (read scope like the PDF; `TechErrorReportSender.preview(report, channel, team, delivery)`: the SAME path as a send — `service.renderPdf` (same ladder, same permit, 429 `PdfBusyException`) with `mailImagePreparer` inside the loader, then the SAME mail cap rule (PDF + inline bytes > `max-mail-mb` → images PDF-only; the PDF alone over the cap → `problem PDF_TOO_LARGE`, body still shown) and `buildFull` with the real file name — NO claim / mail / `notification_logs` / content revision; the response carries `pdf_bytes` + `max_mail_mb`; recipients computed like the send but an empty list is a `problem` code (`TEAM_EMAIL_MISSING` / `EXEC_RECIPIENTS_MISSING`, before `PDF_TOO_LARGE`), never an exception; `cid:` image references become `data:` URIs ONLY in the preview (`inlineCids`, replaced WITH the closing quote — `te-img-tech-ratio` is a prefix of `te-img-tech-ratio-monthly`; the header's `cid:brand-logo` likewise, `inlineBrandLogo`, variant `ok` like the send); the editor runs `ensureSaved()` before opening the preview (a dirty draft would otherwise preview stale content and send new content); delivery = `approvalRequired(ch) ? EXECUTIVE : TEAM`; a grant-only reader gets `to`/`cc` empty + `to_count`/`cc_count` + `recipients_hidden` (people rule). UI: `ReportEditor` "E-postayı önizle" (`te-mail-preview-btn`, next to PDF, for every reader) → `MailPreviewDialog` (subject / To + delivery badge / Cc / attachment + image counts, problem banner, desktop 1040 px ↔ phone 400 px `SegmentedControl`, `iframe sandbox="" srcDoc` `te-mail-frame` `data-width`, "Gönder" only when the editor's own send would be enabled (`onSend` → closes and runs the existing confirm + send), `te.mail.*` keys). **Provenance (same day, user request: "oluşturan kişi, onaylayan kişi bilgileri gözükmeli"):** `TechErrorPdfInput.Provenance` (created by / at, updated by / at, `approvalState` null = no approval flow else none | pending | approved | rejected | stale, approved by / at ONLY for `approved` = APPROVED ∧ `approval_rev` = COALESCE(`content_rev`, 0)) filled by `TechErrorReportService.provenance(r, ch)` inside `pdfInput`; `TechErrorReportMail.provenanceLines` is the single text source for the mail band (`te-mail-provenance` lines: "Oluşturan: A · dd.MM.yyyy HH:mm", "Son düzenleyen: B · …" only when different, "Onaylayan: C · …" or "Onay: <state>", then "Üretim: …") AND the PDF cover (band grows 10 pt per extra line); the old 11-arg constructor (provenance null) keeps the old single "Hazırlayan · Üretim" line byte-identically. The preview response carries `provenance {created_by, created_at, updated_by, updated_at, approval_state, approved_by, approved_at}` for EVERY reader (document provenance, written in the body too — not a people list); the dialog shows the rows (`te-mail-created|updated|approved|approval`), and the report header shows "Oluşturan <date> · <name>" (`te-created`, `te.createdLine`). Gates: `TechErrorReportMailTest.provenanceLines`, `TechErrorReportPdfWriterTest.coverProvenanceLines`, `TechErrorReportServiceTest.provenanceFromRow`. **Review round (same day):** the footer's "why you received this" follows the audience — `TechErrorReportMail.Audience` TEAM (old text, byte-identical) / EXECUTIVE (channel's executive recipient, team in Cc, "+ approved by PO X on …" when the provenance is approved) / APPROVER (approval request mail) via the 6-arg `buildFull`; `TechErrorApprovalService.approvers` really nulls the e-mail of a second PO sharing a mailbox (one request mail, one token; both still approve in-app); the approval link page's `lang` + description follow the request language. Gates: `TechErrorReportControllerTest.mailPreview`, `TechErrorReportSenderTest.previewBuildsWithoutSending` (PDF_TOO_LARGE, sum cap, cid prefix), `TechErrorReportMailTest.footerWhyByAudience`, `TechErrorApprovalServiceTest.approversSharedMailboxDedupe`, `TechErrorMailPreview.test.jsx`, `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ("e-posta önizlemesi", 390/768/1280).
    - **Test mail to self + recipient summary + review fixes (2026-10-17, user requests "bana test gönder" and the module review):** `POST /api/tech-error-reports/reports/{id}/mail-preview/test` (same read gate as the preview — a grant-only reader may use it, the mail goes only to themselves): the SESSION user needs a deliverable address (409 `NO_EMAIL`, checked before the quota), 3 per minute per user per pod (429 `RATE_LIMITED` + `Retry-After`); an attempt that never reached the mail service (PDF queue full → 429, `PDF_TOO_LARGE` → 409) gives the reservation back (`releaseTestMail`), SENT / QUEUED / SKIPPED / FAILED spend it. `TechErrorReportSender.sendTest` renders ONCE through the same private `render()` as `preview()` (same PDF ladder / permit / images / file name / footer audience of the real delivery), subject `[TEST] ` + title, a warning block after the band in the tester's UI language (`testIntro`), PDF attached; NO `claimSend`, `touchContent`, history event, `last_sent_*` or approval check. ONE `notification_logs` row per attempt (trigger `TECH_ERROR_REPORT_TEST`, role `SELF`); the audit `TECH_ERROR_REPORT_TEST_MAIL` has no address and its `reason` is the error CODE (`failureReason`: `PDF_BUSY`, `PDF_TOO_LARGE`, … — also used by `/send`). The preview returns `pdf_too_large` as a separate flag next to `problem` (which names the recipient issue first); `MailPreviewDialog` hides "Bana test gönder" (`te-mail-test`) only on it (old server: `pdf_bytes > max_mail_mb`) and can show the recipient strip and the PDF strip together. **Approval link = in-app gates** (`TechErrorApprovalService.linkDecisionAllowed`): `tech_error_reports.approve/execute` + `tech_error_reports.read/view` (`PermissionService.allows(systemRole, …)`, no session) + the module enabled for the report's team (`TechErrorVisibilityService`: no bean = unrestricted, unreadable = closed); otherwise reason `no_permission` — the page says why, no form, the token is NOT consumed, `TECH_ERROR_APPROVAL_LINK_DENIED reason=no_permission`. Deliberate difference: the link does not exempt a global viewer. **Superseded links:** `request()` no longer deletes the previous request's tokens — `tech_error_approval_tokens.superseded_at` (`patch()` line; NULL = live) is stamped (`supersedeByReportId`), superseded + expired rows of the report are pruned on the next request (`deleteSupersededExpired`); `status()` order used → superseded → expired → not_found → not_pending → approver_inactive → no_permission; `markUsed` stamps only a token that is unused AND not superseded (0 rows → re-read → used / superseded / not_found, no decision); the superseded page also points to deciding in Site Monitor. **Recipient caps never drop silently:** `parseAddressesDetailed` = `{addresses, dropped}` (`MAX_RECIPIENTS` 20; `parseAddresses` / `recipients` byte-identical, Admin → Teams validation unchanged); To overflow → `to_dropped` / history `dropped_recipients`; on executive delivery the Cc is the team list MINUS the executives, then capped at 20 (`ccAddresses`; before the fix it was capped first) → `cc_dropped`, WARN, history `dropped_cc`, strips `te-mail-dropped` / `te-mail-cc-dropped`, history notes `te-history-send-dropped` / `te-history-send-cc-dropped`. **Recipient summary:** `GET /reports/{id}` adds `team_email_count` (≤ 20) + `team_email_dropped`; `RecipientsSummary` (`te-recipients`) under the report title: "Takım adresi: N" (0 → "yok", destructive; dropped → "20 (+N gitmez)", warning), with an approval flow "Yönetici alıcı: M" + the approval-state chip (`ApprovalCard.approvalStateLabelKey` / `APPROVAL_TONE`); a MISSING count draws no chip (unknown ≠ 0). **Phone action bar:** `hooks/useElementHeight` (ResizeObserver, border-box) sets the report editor's bottom padding = bar height + 16 px (`pb-24` only while unmeasured); "E-postayı önizle" stays a button at every width. Gates: `TechErrorReportSenderTest.sendTest_*|recipients|droppedRecipientsAreCountedAndRecorded`, `TechErrorReportControllerTest.mailPreviewTest|mailPreviewTest_failedAttemptsRefundQuota|reportDetailRecipientCounts|mailPreview`, `TechErrorApprovalServiceTest.approvePermissionGate|resendSupersedesOldLinks|supersededBetweenStatusAndMarkUsed|linkNeedsReadPermissionAndModuleVisibility`, `TechErrorApprovalLinkControllerTest.deniedReasons` (own IP per case — the 30/min limiter is shared across the class), `TechErrorReportServiceTest.sendEventCarriesDroppedRecipients`, `useElementHeight.test.js`, `TechErrorMailPreview.test.jsx`, `TechErrorRecipientsSummary.test.jsx`, `TechErrorHistory.test.jsx`, `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ("e-posta önizlemesi" test-mail step; "rapor:" last section ends above the action bar).
    - **Gates:** `TechErrorCalcTest` (truth table, apply, template shape), `TechErrorCalcMigrationTest` (pure + H2 job), `TechErrorReportServiceTest.calcOnSaveAndLivePdf`, `techErrorCalcModel.test.js`, `TechErrorCalc.test.jsx`, `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ("otomatik oranlar", 390/768/1280), `e2e/live/channel-reports.live.spec.js` (step 6).
  - **Gates:** `TechErrorTemplateTest`, `TechErrorValuesTest`, `TechErrorImageValidatorTest`, `TechErrorReportPdfWriterTest`, `TechErrorReportServiceTest` (H2, `NOT_SUPPORTED`: content revision, conditional delete, release claim, stale entity save), `TechErrorReportSenderTest`, `TechErrorReportControllerTest`, `TechErrorReportPostgresTest` (concurrent open → 409 with id, CASE/LIKE release), `EmailSamples` (`report-tech-error-tr|en`), `techErrorModel.test.js`, `TechErrorReports.test.jsx`, `TechErrorReportsReview.test.jsx`, `uactModel.test.js` (tab label), `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` (390/768/1280: fit, no clipping, touch targets, chart grid columns, X → X / Y → Y template).
- **Monthly executive summary (2026-10-10, user request)** — `service/report/executive/*`, `?tab=executive`, `/api/executive-summary[/pdf|/settings|/send-test|/run]`.
  - **Sections are providers:** every `@Component implements ExecutiveSummarySection` is picked up and ordered by `order()` (10 availability, 20 noise, 30 expirations, 40 renewals, 50 `tls-grade` (distribution + month's drops; tier-1 F → critical), 60 `crypto-readiness` (`CryptoInventoryService.summary(null, 10)`; any BROKEN → critical), 70 `data-quality` (`DataQualityService.digest()` + `data_quality_daily` month ends; band → status) — the last three are `snapshot=true`; feature codes print in Turkish in mail/PDF, `ExecutiveSummaryCodeLabelsGateTest` keeps the Java labels equal to `tr.js`). A section returns a generic `SectionResult` (verdicts, KPIs, tables, notes, optional `data`); the mail (`ExecutiveSummaryMail`), the PDF (`ExecutiveSummaryPdfWriter`) and the page render it generically — adding a section needs no other file except TR+EN `exec.<key>.*` keys (`ExecutiveSummaryI18nGateTest` scans the provider sources). Fixed number of aggregate queries per section; inventory / latest certs / team names are read once per summary via `ExecutiveSummaryContext`. Month boundaries are Europe/Istanbul (hourly rollup buckets), never UTC days (daily rollup only as a flagged fallback).
  - **Honest labels:** "SLO" = measured availability (check-weighted, `monitor_check_hourly`) vs the org target, not a formal SLO. Renewal = first sighting of a new fingerprint that EXTENDS validity. **Renewal compliance (2026-10-12, user decision):** `on_time_pct` = renewals made BEFORE expiry ÷ (renewals + certificates that expired IN the month without being renewed, from the fingerprint groups — `RenewalTimelinessSection.lapses`); renewal plans never affect it. "Ahead of target" (`early_pct`, lead left ≥ `renewal-target-days`, 0 = tier lead days) is information only; last minute < 7 days. Sent months show their stored snapshot — "Canlı hesapla" (`live=1`) recomputes with the current rule.
  - **Access:** org summary = global viewer (global admin / AUDIT) AND `executive_summary.view`. Team summary (`?team=<id>`) = also the scoped admin ("müdür") for teams in `viewTeamIds`; USER / TEAM_ADMIN never (even with the matrix grant). No param → org for global viewers, else the first visible team (A→Z); `team=org` without org access → 403. Org settings / test / send = global admin (keys GLOBAL_ONLY); team settings / test / send = global admin or a scoped admin with `canManage(team)`.
  - **Team scope (2026-10-10, user request: "takım bazlı yönetici ayarlaması"):** `ExecutiveSummaryContext` carries `scopeTeamId`; `inventory()` / `latestCerts()` return only rows whose SY OR UG team is the scope team (`allInventory()` / `allLatestCerts()` unfiltered), monitors / alarms filter with `teamInScope` / `domainInScope`; team scope drops the per-team breakdowns and uses team-scope codes (TR+EN `exec.<key>.*`). Org output is byte-identical. `ctx.shared(key, loader)` is a run-wide memo: a delivery run computes org + N teams and reads org-wide raw rows once (`ExecutiveSummaryService.newRunShared`). `ExecutiveSummary.scope` (`org` | `team` + id/name; old snapshots without it read as org) drives mail subject / badge / links (`ex_team`), PDF cover and file name (`fileName(summary)`).
  - **Team recipients and delivery:** `executive_summary_team_settings` (team PK; `enabled` default off, `include_manager` = `teams.manager_id`, `include_team_admins` = AD scoped admins whose manage scope has the team, selected ACTIVE members, extra addresses ≤ 200) — `ExecutiveSummaryTeamService` (fixed queries for any number of teams; passive addresses dropped; note codes `exec.team.note.*`). `/api/executive-summary/teams[/{id}[/send-test|/run]]`, audit `EXECUTIVE_SUMMARY_TEAM_SETTINGS` (run / test / export reuse the existing events with a `team` detail). The same cron sends enabled ACTIVE teams after the org summary, independent of the org switch; each team × month is exactly-once in `executive_summary_team_reports` (UNIQUE team×year×month, same claim/reclaim via the `ExecutiveReportRow` interface and delivery `Slot`s); one team's failure never stops the others; rows older than 24 months are pruned each run; catch-up runs when the org OR any team is on (`existsByEnabledTrue`). Retention: both tables have team-deleted orphan rules. Gates: `ExecutiveSummaryTeamServiceTest`, `ExecutiveSummaryTeamDeliveryTest`, `ExecutiveSummaryServiceTeamScopeTest`, `ExecutiveSummaryControllerTeamTest`, `ExecutiveSummaryTeamMailTest`, the `*TeamScopeTest` section tests.
  - **UI (redesign 2026-10-10):** `components/executive/*` — Tabs "Rapor" / "Alıcılar ve gönderim" (the latter only for configurers; force-mounted, mounted on first visit). Report: scope picker (SearchableSelect) + month, hero (scope chip, status, section-health bar, KPI tiles captioned with their section, key findings), sticky table of contents ≥ 1024 px / scrolling chips below, section cards. Delivery: master list (org + teams, on/off, recipients, last status) → detail (`ExecOrgSettings` / `ExecTeamSettings`); < 1024 px list → detail with a back button; unsaved changes ask before switching. URL `ex_m`, `ex_team`, `ex_live`, `ex_view=delivery`, `ex_sel`; the mail's `ex_cfg=1` opens the delivery view. The tab and menu show for ADMIN (incl. scoped) and AUDIT; the server filters. Gates: `ExecutiveSummaryPage.test.jsx`, `e2e/executive-summary.spec.js` (390/768/1280, both views).
  - **Delivery:** opt-in (default OFF); dynamic cron (`0 0 9 1 * *`) + hourly catch-up (72 h) under `scheduler_lock` "executive-summary"; exactly once per month via `executive_summary_reports` UNIQUE(year, month) + conditional `reclaim` (FAILED/NO_RECIPIENT retried ≤ 3, SENDING never auto-resent — only a MANUAL send may take over a SENDING row claimed ≥ 1 h ago; an unexpected error after the claim finishes the row as FAILED/PARTIAL instead of leaving SENDING; an org-path error never stops the team loop; manual send of the CURRENT, unfinished month is rejected with 400 `month` and the button is disabled — 2026-10-12); BCC chunks of 100 with the PDF; passive users' addresses dropped; every chunk / skip in `notification_logs` (`EXECUTIVE_SUMMARY`, test `EXECUTIVE_SUMMARY_TEST`); system-maintenance mute is NOT applied (same as the other reports). Sent months store `summary_json` and the page shows that snapshot (`source=snapshot`) unless `live=1`.
  - **Correctness rules (2026-10-12):** a month is sendable only after 06:00 Istanbul on the 1st of the NEXT month (the nightly roll-up runs at 03:00): manual send → 400 `month`, scheduled run / catch-up → `DATA_PENDING` (`ExecutiveSummaryService.dataReady`, UI `runHintKey`). Base loads (latest certs, inventory, roll-ups, overview rows, section queries) THROW instead of returning empty, so a read failure is a section ERROR, never NO_DATA or a clean verdict; scheduled / catch-up delivery with an ERROR section finishes FAILED and is retried (sent anyway on the last of `MAX_ATTEMPTS`; manual send always sends). Org recipients with an unreadable global-admin list, or team recipients whose teams / settings / members / managers can't be read, send NOTHING (no claim, catch-up retries; manual team send → `RECIPIENTS_UNAVAILABLE`). Paused inventory domains stay mapped in availability. A month already SENT / PARTIAL / SENDING is not claimable, so a scheduled re-run reads no recipients and writes no "unreadable" trace; that trace is written once per month per pod (review round 5); the scheduled path returns ALREADY_SENT / IN_PROGRESS from the row itself without trying a claim (review round 6). The TLS-grade section's drop log is a base load (throws → section ERROR); only its coverage note is optional. PDF table text wraps (`PdfCanvas.wrapCell`, ≤ 6 lines, long tokens split). Settings forms send only changed fields (`utils/changedFields.js`). A deleted / unknown `ex_team` falls back to the default scope; the toast shows only after it loaded.
- **Admin runtime settings** — `AppSettingsService` (curated key/value, catalog in `AppSettingsCatalog`, live-reloads CORS), `SmtpSettingsService`/`SmtpMailService`, `GeneralSettingsController`, `DatabaseInfoService`, and `SecretCipher`/`SecretToolsService` (AES-GCM decrypt of stored SMTP/LDAP passwords, gated by `SITE_MONITOR_SECRET_KEY`). These settings + secret-tools endpoints are bootstrap-admin only.
- **Secret key rotation (2026-10-11, user request).** `site.monitor.secret-key-previous` (`SITE_MONITOR_SECRET_KEY_PREVIOUS`, Helm `secret.secretKeyPrevious`) is DECRYPT-ONLY: `SecretCipher` encrypts with the current key and decrypts current → previous (GCM tag decides; no key id). The stored format is unchanged (`enc:v1:`) so a rollback still reads values — never introduce a new prefix. `SecretRotationService.LOCATIONS` is the single list of persisted encrypted values (LDAP, SMTP, HTTP/PageSpeed basic auth + headers, keyword headers, scripted env + script versions, push headers app_setting); `SecretLocationsGateTest` fails when a model gains a `*_enc` column or a new file calls `encrypt(` without a location. Status (`GET /api/admin/secret-tools/rotation`) counts current / previous / unreadable per location (keyset pages of 500, ≤ 100k rows); re-encrypt (`POST …/rotation/reencrypt`, `{"confirm":true}`, `scheduler_lock` `secret-reencrypt` via `SchedulerService.callWithSchedulerLock`, 409 codes `SECRET_KEY_NOT_CONFIGURED` / `PREVIOUS_KEY_NOT_CONFIGURED` / `REENCRYPT_RUNNING`) rewrites ONLY previous-key values with a conditional `UPDATE … WHERE id=? AND col=<read value>` (singletons + app_settings also bump `updated_at` so other pods' caches refresh); audit `SECRET_REENCRYPT` carries counts only. OTP: `OtpCodes.matches` also accepts the previous key's sub-key (both HMACs always computed); no sub-key is derived from the public dev key. UI: `admin/secret/SecretRotationPanel` inside Secret tools (phases `dev_key|pending|done|incomplete|done_unreadable|unreadable|count_incomplete|steady`, KPI cards filter the location list, unreadable records listed by name). Procedure: docs/RUNBOOK.md → Anahtar döndürme. Gates: `SecretCipherRotationTest`, `SecretRotationServiceTest` (H2, real SQL), `SecretRotationPostgresTest`, `SecretToolsControllerTest`, `SecretRotationPanel.test.jsx`, `secretRotationModel.test.js`, `e2e/secret-rotation.spec.js`.
  **2026-10-12:** re-encryption resumes each location where the previous run stopped (row cap or the 8-minute budget → `partial`, cursor per pod; the row cap counts WRITE attempts, not rows read — a pod without the cursor skips current-key rows cheaply and still progresses, review round 4); `can_reencrypt` stays on while a count is truncated; with no previous key a location error / truncated count shows `count_incomplete`, never `steady`. Gates: `SecretRotationServiceTest.reencryptResumesAcrossRuns`, `secretRotationModel.test.js`.
- **Diagnostics** — `OpensslDiagnosticsService` / `NetworkDiagnosticsService` / `HstsDiagnosticsService` / `ConnectionDiagnosticsService`, persisted by `DiagnosticHistoryService` (`diagnostic_runs`); surfaced via the DiagnosticsModal under `/api/admin/diagnostics/*`.
- **HTTP end-to-end diagnosis (2026-10-02)** — `POST /api/monitoring/http/{id}/diagnose` (+ `/diagnose/history[/{runId}]`), `HttpDiagnosticsController` + `service/http/diagnose/*` (`RawHttpProbe` raw-socket HTTP/1.1 trace: DNS, TCP/proxy, CONNECT, TLS chain/trust, exact request/response headers, redirect hops, 32 KB text body preview, curl -v transcript; `HttpDiagFindings` code catalogue; `HttpDiagMasker`). Runs the monitor's own route and, when a proxy is configured, the other route in parallel (`PATH_DIFFERS` = the 2026-10-02 node/egress incident in one click), plus `client_check` = the real `HttpCheckerService.checkForDiagnostics` (no CA auto-pin, no trust-failure recording, existing `check()` untouched). Side-effect free: no `http_checks` rows, no alarm evaluation. Gate = `diagnostics.run/execute` + `SessionScope.canOperateTeam` (list rows carry `can_diagnose`); 6/min per user and per monitor, max 4 in flight. History in `diagnostic_runs` (`run_type=HTTP_DIAG`, domain `http-monitor:<id>`, so the admin cert history never lists them), body previews never stored. Secrets (auth/cookie/encrypted custom headers, Set-Cookie values, proxy/basic-auth passwords) are masked everywhere incl. the transcript. UI: `components/http/diagnose/*` (lazy dialog from the HTTP detail header "Uçtan uca tanıla" and the failure-diagnosis footer; never auto-runs; `hdx=<runId>` opens a stored run). A new finding code needs `httpdx.finding.<CODE>.title|body` in tr.js + en.js (`HttpDiagFindingsI18nGateTest`); `params.reason` variants use `…<part>.<reason>` keys.
- **Certificate window → "Değişiklikler" tab (2026-10-05, user request: "who added / changed a certificate monitor, when").**
  - **Source:** no new data source. Inventory add / edit / delete / restore are already written to the monitor change log (`MonitorHistoryService.INVENTORY`) and the audit log.
  - **UI:** `certmodal/CertChangesTab.jsx` resolves the inventory record by domain (`/admin/inventory/by-domain`) and shows an "Ekleyen / Son güncelleyen" card (`created_*` / `updated_*` columns), plus the shared `ChangeHistoryTab` (kind `inventory`, field-level before → after). Team names come from `/api/teams/directory`.
  - **Visibility:** same rule as the Inventory drawer — the tab is hidden for another team's (read-only) record and in preview mode.
  - **Legacy:** records older than the change log show only the added / last-updated card.
  - **Gates:** `CertChangesTab.test.jsx`, `e2e/cert-detail-stability.spec.js` (9 tabs, the changes tab fits at 390 / 768 / 1440).
- **Uploaded (manual) certificates (2026-10-06, user request: certificates with no network endpoint — JKS keystores/truststores on OCP, vendor PEMs — tracked from an uploaded file).**
  - **Model:** a normal `certificate_inventory` row with `cert_source='MANUAL'` (NULL = network row, behaviour byte-identical; `isManual()`); the key is a tracking key (`ManualCertificateKeys`, `^(\*\.)?[a-z0-9]([a-z0-9._-]{0,251}[a-z0-9])?$`, class-level `@InventoryDomainKey` keeps the old regex for network rows). Versions in `manual_certificate_versions` (`is_current`, one current per row — partial unique index on PostgreSQL; PUBLIC chain PEM only). Private keys and passwords are NEVER stored, logged, audited or returned.
  - **Private keys and keystore passwords NEVER leave the browser (2026-10-08, user decision: "keystore yüklemesinde kesinlikle private key için bir yükleme yapmayalım").** The wizard extracts the public certificates client-side (`components/manualcert/extract/*`, Web Worker, lazy chunk; own BER/PKCS#12 walker that decrypts ONLY cert bags — PBES2 AES / legacy 3DES + RC2 via `node-forge` primitives, never forge's pkcs12/RSA modules; own JKS/JCEKS reader that skips protected key bytes; `fflate` for ZIP; BKS/UBER unsupported with a keytool guide) and every upload call sends only the `extracted` JSON part (`{format, file_name, size_bytes, entries[{alias,key_entry,certs[b64 DER]}], csr_pem[], private_keys_removed}`), validated strictly by `ExtractedUpload` (unknown fields such as `password` rejected, whitelist formats, ≤ 200 certs, DER X.509 only, no "PRIVATE KEY"). The legacy raw `file`/`text` path accepts certificate-only PEM/DER/PKCS#7/ZIP and rejects PKCS#12, JKS/JCEKS/BKS and anything with a private key with 400 `PRIVATE_KEY_NOT_ACCEPTED`; there is no server-side keystore/password code any more. Warning `PRIVATE_KEY_KEPT_LOCAL {count}`. Never add a code path that sends a key or a keystore password to the server.
  - **One tracked certificate per chain (2026-10-07, user decision):** `ManualCertificateAnalyzer` folds every certificate that appears in another one's built chain into that head; `entries` = chain heads only (leaf+int+root ⇒ 1, int+root ⇒ 1, two unrelated roots ⇒ 2), each with `preview` (offline SSL-tab map) and `certificate_count`. A folded `ref` is rejected with 400 on create / batch / renew. Never reintroduce one record per chain member.
  - **Pipeline:** `loadDomainsFromInventory` drops manual rows (network sweep, outage arithmetic and metrics unchanged). `SchedulerService.runManualCertificateStep` runs after the hourly / stale / full runs under its own `scheduler_lock` key `manual-cert-sweep`; `ManualCertificateEvaluationService` builds the SAME result map offline (`via=upload`, leaf-only ⇒ `chain_status=UNKNOWN`, trust only for a complete chain, revocation cached 6 h) → `CertificateService.saveResult` → `processResults`. Same thresholds / tiers / routing / re-alerts; HOSTNAME_MISMATCH is never raised for manual rows. Upload, renew, `/evaluate`, `/check/{domain}` and `/health/refresh` are manual triggers: recovery-only (`resolveVerifiedStaleCertAlerts`), never a new alarm.
  - **Skipped for manual rows:** uptime sweep/overview, derived Port/DNS provisioning + sweep gates, `DomainExpiryRefreshService`, weekly availability, `/check-preview` and `/admin/diagnostics/*` (409 `MANUAL_CERT`), network-only health rows (NA, `notApplicableManual`), deployment/auto-pin SAN gates, storm denominators (`countNetworkActive*`, `SQL_ACTIVE_BY_TEAM`). Purge paths delete versions.
  - **API:** `/api/manual-certs` (`analyze`, create, `batch` ≤ 20, `{id}/versions` renew with `OLDER_THAN_CURRENT` / `SAME_CERTIFICATE` (`allow_same=true` stores an identical certificate as v+1 — "Yine de yükle", never bypasses OLDER for a different cert), `DELETE {id}/versions/{vid}` permanent delete of a NON-current version (409 `CURRENT_VERSION`, numbers kept, audit `CERT_MANUAL_VERSION_DELETE`), list, detail, PEM download, `evaluate`); `GET /check-preview/{domain}` on a manual row = offline map of the current version (no row, no alarm)); write = `inventory.crud` + team scope (create reuses `AdminController.createInventoryRecord`); audit `CERT_MANUAL_UPLOAD` / `CERT_MANUAL_RENEW`. DTO/list rows carry `cert_source`, `manual_version`, `manual_uploaded_at` only for manual rows (omitted otherwise). The alarm mail adds a "Sertifika Kaynağı" line for manual rows only.
  - **UI:** `?tab=manualcerts` (`components/manualcert/*`: guidance card — COLLAPSED by default, also on an empty list (user decision 2026-10-07), open state remembered in `sm.mcert.guide` — list, `UploadWizard` file → review → track/renew → result), "Dosyadan sertifika ekle" in the Dashboard/Inventory "Domain Ekle" split button, `ManualCertBadge` on card/table/inventory/modal, CertificateModal manual mode (SSL tab = offline preview with the SAME verdict / cert+trust groups / `SslChainView` as network rows, no connection group or hostname row; no diagnose; no Grafik tab; "Yeniden değerlendir"; Versions tab with "Sil" on old versions and "Görüntüle"). **Browser-style hierarchy viewer (2026-10-08):** `GET /api/manual-certs/{id}/versions/{vid}/chain` (read gate as detail; offline from `chain_pem`, no writes) returns nodes ROOT FIRST (`ManualCertificateHierarchy`: role root|intermediate|leaf, `issuer_missing` when the top is not self-signed, per-node fields + single-cert `pem`); `certmodal/CertHierarchyView` (tree role, keyboard, details pane, per-node PEM download) behind the SSL tab's "Zincir | Hiyerarşi (tarayıcı gibi)" switch (chain view stays default; network rows since 2026-10-12, see "Network certificate hierarchy") and the Versions "Görüntüle" dialog; i18n `chier.*`, `InventoryFormModal` manual mode (network fields hidden; the tracking key is EDITABLE since 2026-10-07 — `trackingKeyError` client rule, rename confirm, server `manualKeyForUpdate` + rename of latest/checks/alerts/notes; versions are keyed by inventory id).
  - **Adding a warning code:** TR+EN `mcert.warn.<CODE>` (`ManualCertWarningI18nGateTest`). Gates: `CertificateFileParserTest`, `ManualCertificateSchedulerSplitTest`, `ManualCertificateEvaluationServiceTest`, `ManualCertificateControllerTest`, `CertificateServiceManualSourceTest`, `InventoryNetworkCountQueriesTest`, `e2e/manual-cert.spec.js`.
- **Network certificate hierarchy (2026-10-12, user request: "otomatik çekilen sertifikalarda da tarayıcı gibi hiyerarşi; Root / Intermediate / Leaf net, her birinin bitiş tarihi ayrı ayrı; mevcut işlevler bozulmasın").**
  - **Why live:** network rows store only chain METADATA (`latest_checks.chain_details`, wiped to `[]` on an error check); no PEM, no root. The hierarchy therefore reads the chain on demand.
  - **Endpoint:** `GET /api/certificates/{domain}/hierarchy` (`CertificateHierarchyController`). Gate = `inventory.list/view` + an INVENTORY row (ad-hoc host → 404) + the SAME read rule as the SSL tab preview (`canViewMonitoring` on SY or UG, 7/24 operator, org-wide read; else 403). A manual row → 409 `MANUAL_CERT` (it has its own view). 4 in flight (checked FIRST, so a busy 429 does not spend the quota), then 6 per minute per user × domain → 429 `RATE_LIMITED`; connection / handshake failure (IOException) → 502 `HIERARCHY_UNREACHABLE`, unresolvable name → 502 `DNS_UNRESOLVABLE` (not a security block), SSRF block → 400 `BLOCKED`, any other exception → 500 `HIERARCHY_FAILED` + ERROR log (exception text never leaves the server). Optional `tls_mode` (browser|default) and `via` (proxy|direct) = the route the SSL tab's preview just succeeded on (`tls_mode_used` / `via`), so hosts that pass only on the check's fallback work too; other values are ignored and the proxy is still used only when configured and not NO_PROXY. Read-only: no row, cache, alarm or audit.
  - **Capture:** `CertificateCheckerService.capturePresentedChain` = the check's own route (SSRF guard, `use_proxy ∧ proxy ∧ ¬NO_PROXY`, multi-A, SNI, browser TLS mode, record timeout), trust-all read, one attempt, no OCSP / CRL / DNS / HSTS. Total time (connect + handshake) ≤ `CAPTURE_TOTAL_SEC` 75 s — under the client's 90 s GET timeout; the deadline scheduler removes cancelled tasks at once. `via=direct` is honoured only when the check could really fall back (`retryFallbackEnabled` = retry-fallback ∧ transient retry ∧ ≥ 2 attempts).
  - **Build (browser / PKIX-like):** `service/certhierarchy/NetworkCertificateHierarchy` (pure). From the leaf up, each step tries (1) a SELF-SIGNED trust anchor (`TrustEvaluator.trustAnchors()`: system store, then the corporate CA bundle) that signs the current cert — the path ENDS there (cross-signed roots: the store root is shown, the server's cross-sign becomes an extra; the server's identical copy is used when it sent one), (2) an issuer among the sent certs (name + SIGNATURE, order-independent), (3) a non-self-signed trust-store intermediate (the walk continues). Several candidates → currently valid first, then latest `notAfter`. A self-signed STORE anchor is accepted without CA extensions (PKIX does not check the anchor — old corporate roots); store intermediates and server-sent issuers must be CAs (`canIssue`). A signature algorithm this JVM can't verify counts as "signed" when the names match (same rule as `ManualCertificateChains.isSelfSigned`) — for the PATH only: such a link sets `signature_unverified: true` and `root_trusted: false`, and the UI shows the root as "unknown" (`chier.net.why.signatureUnverified`), never green (review round 6). Depth ≤ 10, input ≤ 20, cycle-safe. Response = the manual `ManualCertificateHierarchy` node format (+ per node `source` server|trust_store, `trust_store` system|corporate) + `root_source` (`trust_store|server|self_signed`, null when the top is not self-signed), `root_trusted` (the root IS in a store — a server-sent root that no store holds is NOT trusted), `missing_kind` (`intermediate` = the server sent only the leaf, `anchor` = the chain ends at a store intermediate, `root`), `order_ok`, `presented_count`, `extra_certificates` (≤ 10, deduped), `via`, `port`, `checked_at`. Negative `days_remaining` is kept.
  - **UI:** the SSL tab's "Zincir | Hiyerarşi (tarayıcı gibi)" switch now also shows on network rows when `CertificateModal` passes `networkHierarchyDomain` (not in preview mode, not manual); "Zincir" (SslChainView) stays the default and is unchanged. `certmodal/NetworkCertHierarchy` (lazy) = summary card (root source badge + why, sent count, wrong-order warning, proxy, read time) + `CertHierarchyView` + collapsible extra certificates. The result is cached per preview response in a MODULE-level WeakMap keyed by the `data` object (the in-flight request is shared, errors are not cached), so toggling views or switching window tabs never reconnects and "Yeniden kontrol et" refreshes it. `networkHierarchyModel`: `issuer_missing: true` always means "missing" (a store intermediate is never shown as a root); an absent field is unknown; the root badge is green ONLY for a store root or a server root with `root_trusted: true`; `missing_kind` picks the badge / why / placeholder text, and the placeholder shows the expected issuer (`missingIssuerLabel`).
  - **Tree (both manual and network):** every row shows a role badge (`chier.role.*`: root primary, intermediate secondary, leaf outline), the EXPIRY DATE (`chier.expiresOn`) and the days-left badge; names wrap (no `truncate`); a trust-store root carries a "Güven deposundan" badge and a "Kaynak" row in the details. Network wording via `missingRootText` / `emptyText` props.
  - **Gates:** `NetworkCertificateHierarchyTest` (cross-sign, store intermediate, untrusted server root, missing intermediate, valid-anchor preference), `CertificateHierarchyControllerTest`, `NetworkCertHierarchy.test.jsx`, `networkHierarchyModel.test.js`, `CertHierarchyView.test.jsx`, `CertHierarchyEntryPoints.test.jsx` (no prop → no switch), `e2e/cert-hierarchy-network.spec.js` (390/768/1280: default chain, roles + expiry per row, no clipping / overflow, one request across toggles, ≥ 40 px rows).
  - **Review round 4:** only a CA (basicConstraints, keyCertSign when keyUsage is present; a v1 cert only when self-signed) can be an issuer (`canIssue`). A sent certificate named like the top's issuer that cannot be used → `missing_kind=wrong_intermediate`; a lone CA-flagged server cert with no issuer → `intermediate`. `trust_store_readable:false` (system store unreadable) makes the root badge "unknown". `via=direct` is honoured only if the record is not proxied or the check's retry fallback is on; `via=proxy` needs the record's proxy or (fallback ∧ auto proxy fallback). Per-user in-flight cap 2 (`MAX_PER_USER_CONCURRENT`) before the pod-wide cap; the capture uses the SSRF-validated addresses and a 2× timeout socket deadline.
- **Keyword failure diagnosis + end-to-end diagnosis (2026-10-04, user request).**
  - **Every check stores response meta:** `final_url` (masked), `redirect_count`, `content_type`, `body_bytes`, `body_truncated`, `charset`, `via`.
  - **Failed checks also store:**
    - `failure_reason` (`KeywordFailureClassifier.CODES`; network errors via `HttpFailureDiagnostics.classify`);
    - `failure_detail` (Turkish, ≤ 500 chars);
    - `hints` (`KeywordBodyAnalyzer.HINT_CODES`, JSON in the column, a list in the API);
    - a masked `excerpt` (≤ 600 visible chars).
    - `error` is clipped to 255 chars — a longer error used to fail the row insert.
  - **One mapper:** scheduled and manual checks map through `KeywordResultMapper` only (expectation-aware `KeywordCheckerService.check(…, Expectation)`). `ok` / `found` / alarm decisions are unchanged, and manual checks still never alarm.
  - **Diagnosis endpoint:** `POST /api/monitoring/keyword/{id}/diagnose` (+ history) mirrors HTTP diagnosis:
    - `RawHttpProbe` with the keyword User-Agent and `captureBody`;
    - own route + other route;
    - real-checker `client_check`;
    - a keyword analysis block.
  - **Access and limits:** gate `diagnostics.run/execute` + `canOperateTeam` (`can_diagnose`); 6/min per user and per monitor, 4 in flight (its own limiter, separate from HTTP).
  - **History:** `diagnostic_runs` `KEYWORD_DIAG` / `keyword-monitor:<id>`; no body previews or contexts stored.
  - **UI:** `keyword/KeywordCheckFailure.jsx` (history rows), `keyword/diagnose/*` (variant of `HttpDiagnoseDialog`; without a variant the HTTP output is identical), deep link `kdx`.
  - **Adding a code:** TR+EN `kwfail.<C>.short|why|effect|fix`, `kwhint.<C>.title|cause|effect|fix` or `kwdx.finding.<C>.title|body` (`KeywordDiagFindingsI18nGateTest`).
  - **Form "Test" (`POST /keyword/test`):** calls the expectation-aware check and returns the same diagnosis keys (only when the condition is NOT met); the form renders the history `KeywordFailurePanel` (no diagnose button — unsaved monitor).
- **Failure diagnosis + end-to-end diagnosis for the other types (2026-10-05, user request).**
  - **Stored reason:** `service/failure/` — `CheckFailureReason` (code catalogue with a Phase), `CheckFailure` (masked detail map, compact JSON ≤ 4000 chars), `CheckFailureClassifier` (`forException` reuses `HttpFailureDiagnostics.classify`, plus DNS rcode / ping / HTTP status / page resources / domain).
    - Columns `failure_reason` + `failure_detail` on ping/port/dns_records/page/pagespeed/uptime/domain checks, plus `dns_records.error`, `certificate_checks.error_stage|resolved_ips` (literal `patch()` lines).
    - Classification is METADATA only: it never changes ok/up/open/status, error text, alarms, notifications or DNS change detection (`DnsCheckerService.applyFailure` runs only when `!success` and never touches value/changed/manual). It never throws and adds no query.
    - Every save site copies it (sweep in `SchedulerService`, manual checks in `MonitoringController`, `DomainCheckerService`, `CertificateService`) — `CheckFailurePersistenceWiringTest`.
  - **History UI:** `components/checks/` (`checkFailureModel.failureOf(type,row)` — server code first, legacy rows inferred from the card classifiers with `legacy:true`; `CheckFailurePanel` / `CheckFailureCell`). DNS history: failed lookups have `status=fail` + `counts.errors` (opt-in `errorTile` on `CheckHistoryTab`).
  - **Diagnose endpoints:** `POST /api/monitoring/{ping|port|dns}/{id}/diagnose` (`NetworkMonitorDiagnosticsController`, `service/diagnose/*`, step contract via `NetDiagSupport.envelope`) and `/{page|pagespeed}/{id}/diagnose` (`PageDiagnosticsController`, `service/page/diagnose/*`, `RawHttpProbe` with a `Profile` = the real checker's headers). Each has `…/diagnose/history[/{runId}]`.
    - Same rules as HTTP/keyword: `diagnostics.run/execute` + `canOperateTeam` on the effective team (list rows carry `can_diagnose` via `MonitoringController.withDiagnoseFlag`), 6/min per user and per monitor, 4 in flight, side-effect free (no check rows, no alarm evaluation).
    - History: `diagnostic_runs` `PING|PORT|DNS|PAGE|PAGESPEED_DIAG`, key `<type>-monitor:<id>`. `/api/admin/diagnostics/history/{id}` 404s every monitor run type (`NetDiagnosticsHistory.MONITOR_RUN_TYPES`).
    - Audit `*_DIAGNOSTICS_RUN` (MONITOR).
  - **UI:** `components/diagnose/NetDiagnoseDialog` (ping/port/dns; deep links `pgdx`/`ptdx`/`dndx`) and `HttpDiagnoseDialog` variants in `components/page|pagespeed/diagnose/` (`pidx`/`psdx`).
  - **Access fixes:** Domain "Sorun Tanıla", Uptime and the cert modal's diagnose button follow `diagnostics.run` + team scope, not the role. `domain-expiry` also accepts a standalone domain monitor the user can operate.
  - **Adding a code:** TR+EN `chkfail.<C>.short|why|effect|fix` (`CheckFailureI18nGateTest`), `ndx.finding.<C>.title|body` (`NetDiagFindingsI18nGateTest`), `pgdx.finding.*` / `psdx.finding.*` (`PageDiagFindingsI18nGateTest`).
- **Team data quality score (2026-10-10, user request: "sahipsiz kayıt, eksik iletişim, yanlış katman, 7/24'e bildirilmeyen kritik izleme → tek skor + düzeltme listesi").**
  - **Rules:** `service/quality/DataQualityRule` is the single catalogue (code, scope, severity; weight = HIGH 3 / MEDIUM 2 / LOW 1). Ownership rules (`INV_NO_TEAM`, `MON_NO_TEAM`: no team, deleted team or PASSIVE team) never enter a team score — only the org score and the "Ownerless" bucket. Hygiene rules reuse `InventoryHygieneService` codes (`no_contacts`, `never_checked`, `stale`, `error`; failing = current error AND ≥ 3 errors in 7 days). `NOC_CRITICAL_UNCOVERED` reuses `NocCoverageService.reason` for tier 1–2 inventory and applies only while 7/24 is usable (active addressed group + SSL type on), otherwise an org note. Tier heuristics (`TierHeuristics`) flag only strong contradictions (EV on tier 3–4, whole-word test token on tier 1–2, internal cert on tier 1). Team address/escalation rules apply only to teams with ≥ 1 active monitored item.
  - **Score:** per rule health = 1 − failing/eligible; score = round(100 × Σ w·health / Σ w) over applicable rules (`DataQualityScore`, size-independent; no applicable rule → null/`NO_DATA`). Bands ≥ 90 / ≥ 75 / ≥ 50 / below.
  - **Cost:** `DataQualitySource` loads everything org-wide in a FIXED number of queries (12 JDBC + 4 JPA + hygiene + NOC config; `DataQualityQueriesTest.fixedQueryCount`), memoised 60 s (`site.monitor.data-quality.cache-ms`), `?fresh=1` at most every 5 s. Never add a per-team or per-row query.
  - **API/scope:** `GET /api/data-quality` + `/teams/{id|unassigned}`, gate `data_quality.view/view` (PermissionCatalog, all roles by default). Read scope = monitoring read (`canViewMonitoring`; 7/24 operators see all); Ownerless bucket only for `seesAllMonitoring`; row `can_edit` uses each record's OWN write gate (`inventoryWriteTest` / `canOperateTeam` / `canManage`). No user ids in the payload (item details are under `facts`, not `detail`). `DataQualityService.digest()` is the reusable unfiltered summary for other reports.
  - **Trend:** `data_quality_daily` (day × bucket, team_key 0 = org, −1 = ownerless) written by `DataQualitySnapshotJob` (hourly tick, `scheduler_lock` `data-quality-snapshot`, `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`, self-prunes > 120 days; RetentionCatalog BOUNDED).
  - **Partial data (2026-10-12):** required inputs (teams, members, escalation, notification groups, inventory, monitors, pause history) THROW (`DataQualitySource.required`) — no score and no daily snapshot from partial data; optional inputs (hygiene, scan errors, 7/24 config) degrade to "unknown". The snapshot of day D is written at 00:25 on D, i.e. it reflects the END of D-1: a month end is the snapshot of the 1st of the next month (`monthEnds`, labels shifted back one day).
  - **UI:** `?tab=dataquality` (Reports group; `components/dataquality/*`, URL prefix `dq_`; redesign 2026-10-10): org card (`DataQualityOverview.OrgHero`: ring + band + 7-day delta + counts | wide 30-day trend, full-width band distribution), costliest rules + team comparison (top / priority teams, `TeamLeaders`, hidden with < 2 scored teams), ranking card (band tiles, search, sort; table ↔ cards by container width), fix list modal grouped by rule ordered by points lost with a team-vs-org benchmark and rule counts on the severity filter; deep links via `linkFor` (inventory drawer / cert window `open=noc|cert` / monitor detail / Admin teams or contacts). Adding a rule: enum + `dataQualityCodes.js` (same order) + TR/EN `dq.rule.<CODE>.title|why|fix` (`DataQualityRuleI18nGateTest`) + `linkFor` if it needs a new target.
- **TLS configuration grade A+–F (2026-10-10, user request: "protokol, şifre takımları, OCSP stapling, HSTS ve anahtar boyu tek notta; kartta rozet; not düşünce uyarı").**
  - **Rubric (single source `service/tlsgrade/TlsGradeRules`):** caps, no invented score. Starts at A+, each finding caps the grade, result = lowest cap; every grade carries reason codes. F: expired / hostname mismatch / untrusted / revoked / broken chain / weak key / SHA-1 / NULL-anon-EXPORT cipher. D: RC4/3DES negotiated or ≥ 2 C-level reasons (`MULTIPLE_SERIOUS`). C: no TLS 1.2, weak cipher accepted. B: TLS 1.0/1.1 on, no PFS, CBC chosen. A (no A+): no TLS 1.3, HSTS missing/short/unchecked, no OCSP stapling, profile pending/failed/partial. Unknown never lowers the grade but A+ needs proof. Reuses `CertificateHealthRules` + the HSTS 180-day threshold. Manual (uploaded) certs are NOT graded (`not_applicable`).
  - **TLS profile probe (`TlsHelloProbe` + `TlsProfileProbeService`):** raw ClientHello per version (1.0–1.3), TLS 1.2 `status_request` (stapling = CertificateStatus seen), weak-cipher-only hello; never completes a handshake, never touches JVM security properties; bounded (5 s per probe, 30 s per endpoint, 64 KB / 64 records). SSRF-validated; proxy decision identical to the cert check (`use_proxy` ∧ proxy ∧ ¬NO_PROXY). Unknown stays UNKNOWN. Table `tls_profiles` (domain PK).
  - **Job (`TlsProfileJobService`):** sweep leader only + `scheduler_lock` `tls-profile`; tick 10 min, ≤ 40 due endpoints (24 h interval, failed retried after 6 h), own 4-thread pool, 8 min tick budget. Never on the hourly sweep, never from "Check now". Keys `site.monitor.tls-profile.*` (`enabled` default true), `site.monitor.tls-grade.drop-notice-days` (7).
  - **Drops:** `tls_grade_status` (last grade per inventory id) + `tls_grade_changes` (log, series-certificate retention); the job tick reconciles grades from the cached `getAllLatest` rows, writes only on change, activity `TLS_GRADE_DROPPED`. Still NOT an alarm (no alert type; a real alarm would need a new type wired everywhere `HTTP_SLOW` is).
  - **Drop notification (2026-10-11, user request: "TLS notu düştüğünde yalnız uygulama içinde görünüyor"):** a DROP row is born `notify_state=PENDING` (REFINE/RISE never). After reconcile, every job tick runs `TlsGradeDropNotifier.notifyPending()` under the `tls-profile` lock: conditional claim PENDING→SENDING (`transitionNotifyState`, at-most-once; SENDING never resent), then per owning team (SY and UG separately, each sees only its own rows) ONE summary mail (`TlsGradeDropMail`, deep link to the cert window) to `teamEmailsForMonitor` + a team push (`enqueueTeamNoticeLocalized`, trigger `TLS_GRADE_DROP`), one `notification_logs` row per team (alertEventId 0). Final states `SENT` / `SKIPPED: bildirim kapalı | eşik üstü not | sistem bakımı | takım yok | alıcı yok` (+ `notified_at`). Keys `site.monitor.tls-grade.drop-notify` (true) and `…drop-notify-min-grade` (A: new grade ≤ this is notified), group `tls-grade`. Off → pending rows are marked off, never sent later; system-maintenance mute applies. Pre-feature rows (null state) are never sent. A team / recipient READ failure is not final (review round 5): the rows go back to PENDING and are retried on the next ticks (≤ `READ_RETRY_MAX` 6 per pod), then `FAILED`; every decision — including an unexpected send error — writes a `notification_logs` row (`FAILED: alıcılar okunamadı`). The SENDING→PENDING retry transition runs AFTER the round's results are written, so a failing transition never turns delivered rows into "interrupted"; retry counters are dropped for every row that ended (review round 6). Known limit: the stale-SENDING sweep ages rows by `changed_at`, so it relies on the `tls-profile` lock not expiring mid-tick. `/api/tls-grade/drops` rows carry `notify` (UI code via `TlsGradeDropNotifier.uiCode`) + `notified_at`; the "Recent drops" dialog shows it as a badge. Gates: `TlsGradeDropNotifierTest`, `TlsGradeServiceTest.dropsCarryNotifyCode`, `TlsProfileJobServiceTest.tickNotifiesPendingDropsAfterReconcile`, `TlsGradeOverview.test.jsx`, `e2e/tls-grade.spec.js`.
  - **2026-10-12:** an exception after the claim, or a SENDING row older than 1 h, becomes `FAILED: yarıda kaldı` (UI `interrupted`, never resent). A manual rescan only saves the profile and evicts caches — the next tick reconciles under the `tls-profile` lock from a fresh list (`cert-latest` is evicted before every reconcile; an interrupted tick skips reconcile and notify). A grade change explained only by HSTS becoming unmeasured (`HSTS_NOT_CHECKED` added) is REFINE, not DROP (`onlyMeasurementGapAdded`). `/api/tls-grade/drops` filters the viewer's teams in SQL (`findRecentForTeams`).
  - **API:** list rows `tls_grade`, `tls_grade_reasons` (codes), `tls_grade_drop` (NON_NULL/NON_EMPTY — absent when not graded); `/certificates/list` `filter_grade`, facet `grades`, sort `tls_grade`, CSV `tls_grade(_reasons)`; `GET /api/certificates/{d}/tls-grade` (health read gate), `POST …/tls-grade/rescan` (`diagnostics.run` + `canOperateTeam`, 3/min per user and domain, 2 in flight, audit `CERT_TLS_PROFILE_RESCAN`, 409 `MANUAL_CERT`), `GET /api/tls-grade/drops` (monitoring scope + profile coverage).
  - **UI (`components/tlsgrade/*`):** `TlsGradeBadge` (card status row + All Certificates column; tap → "Neden B?"), `TlsGradeSection` (top of the Health tab), `TlsGradeOverview` (All Certificates distribution chips + "Recent drops" dialog). The UI never computes a grade; `tlsGradeCodes.js` mirrors codes + caps.
  - **Adding a reason code:** enum in `TlsGradeRules.Reason`, `tlsGradeCodes.js` (same order + cap), TR+EN `tlsg.reason.<C>.title|why|fix` (`TlsGradeI18nGateTest`, `tlsGradeModel.test.js`). Gates: `TlsGradeRulesTest` (truth table), `TlsHelloProbeTest` (fake server), `TlsProfileProbeServiceTest`, `TlsProfileJobServiceTest`, `TlsGradeServiceTest`, `TlsGradeControllerTest`, `CertificateServiceTlsGradeTest`, `e2e/tls-grade.spec.js` (390/768/1280).

### Deployment specifics
- **K8s manifests** (`k8s/`): **LEGACY, unmaintained, reference only** — `helm/site-monitor/` is the single source of truth for every environment (CI lints only the chart). The manifests have drifted (no `SITE_MONITOR_SECRET_KEY`, dead env keys, `certmonitor`↔`sitemonitor` DB names, registry-less image, no `/var/log` volume); `kubectl apply` as-is will not start a working app. Known incompatibilities: `k8s/README.md`. Prod deploy command: `docs/PROD_DEPLOY_CHECKLIST.md`.
- **Helm chart** (`helm/site-monitor/`) is the authoritative deploy path (`k8s/` is legacy, see `k8s/README.md`). The bundled Bitnami PostgreSQL subchart was removed — prod uses an external/managed DB. Prod deploy command and checklist: `docs/PROD_DEPLOY_CHECKLIST.md` (never `--reuse-values`; `SITE_MONITOR_SECRET_KEY` must be carried over UNCHANGED or every encrypted secret becomes unreadable). Values split per environment under `environments/` (`develop.yaml` / `release.yaml` / `master.yaml`). `develop.yaml` runs 1 replica, email OFF, 2h scan, HPA OFF — useful diff to copy from when setting up a new lower env. `postgresql.primary.extendedConfiguration` preloads `shared_preload_libraries = 'pg_stat_statements'` so the DB-wide query analytics light up (needs a `helm upgrade` + DB restart in prod; the app falls back gracefully until then).
- **Dockerfile**: 3 stages — `node:24-alpine` (frontend) → `maven:3.9` (backend) → `eclipse-temurin:25-jre-alpine` (runtime), every base image pinned to an exact tag + digest (refresh instructions at the top of the file). Container-aware JVM (`-XX:+UseContainerSupport`, heap ≈ 75%), in-pod debug tools (`curl bash dig`), healthcheck via `wget /health`. Final image runs as `appuser:1000`.
- **k6 smoke** (`perf/k6-smoke.js`): 50 VU × 30s against `/health` + `/api/system/network-status`. SLA: error rate < 1%, p95 < 500 ms, 99% checks pass. Auth-gated endpoints returning `401` are treated as healthy (gate working).
- **Local DB**: `data/` holds a SQLite file + WAL/SHM for dev runs (gitignored). Prod uses PostgreSQL exclusively.

### Timezone
Backend stores timestamps as UTC (audit, alerts, notifications). Log timestamps are localized via `LOG_TIMEZONE` (default `Europe/Istanbul`). Frontend formats with `date-fns` against Europe/Istanbul (UTC+3, no DST). When doing date arithmetic, use `setUTC*` to avoid the 3-hour drift trap.

## Before a feature counts as done (2026-10-12, user decision: "hata yapmayalım")

Two review rounds after 20.123.0 found 26 + 17 real bugs. None was on the happy path, every one passed the existing tests, and they came from the same few blind spots. Every new feature AND every fix goes through this section BEFORE it is pushed. For each checklist item that applies, write the code that handles it AND a test that proves it.

**Process**
- Small batches: at most one or two features per release.
- Adversarial review BEFORE the first push: fresh eyes per area asking "how does this break?", verify each finding in the code, fix, then release. Fixes get the same review — 6 of the 17 second-round bugs were inside first-round fixes.
- Gates (2026-10-16, user decision: "daha hızlı ve titiz" — branch + CI for the full suites, layer-based local gates):
  - **Locally, one after another (never in parallel):** the targeted tests of every touched area (vitest files + the area's e2e spec + backend test classes) AND the gate of each CHANGED layer — backend changed → `mvn -B clean verify` (a test failure stops the build before `jacoco:check`, so class floors stay hidden until the tests are green; if the log's last `[SHUTDOWN] JVM: heapUsed` is ≥ 90 %, raise the surefire heap); frontend changed → `npm run build`. An unchanged layer is not re-run locally.
  - **Full vitest + full Playwright + backend verify run on CI, never skipped:** commit on a `feat/…` / `fix/…` branch, `gh pr create --base main` (CI runs on `pull_request` to main, 4 shards, ~15 min), and only when the PR's CI is green bring the SAME commit to main (`git push origin HEAD:main`, fast-forward — the conventional subject drives the release bump). main never goes red; Release waits for main's own CI run of that commit.
  - New requests that arrive while a release is in its gates go to the queue — the release's scope is frozen once its gates start.

**Failure-mode checklist** — ask every question; one test per item that applies:
1. **Several pods.** Does the job run once per cluster (`scheduler_lock`, cluster schedule round, sweep-leader fence)? Does manual triggering use the same lock? Is in-memory per-pod state still valid after another pod writes the shared row (prune / merge by timestamp)?
2. **Interrupted halfway.** Pod dies or an exception happens after a claim: can a row stay in an intermediate state (SENDING, PENDING, CLAIMED) forever? Claim → try/catch → final state; only manual paths may take over a stale claim.
3. **Restart / shutdown.** Background work stops before processing results when shutdown starts. Slow work is off the startup path. One-time data fixes use a `schema_patch_markers` marker written only on success — never gate on "rows changed in this boot" (an earlier release may already have changed them).
4. **Existing data.** What does the new code do with rows written by the previous release: null new columns, data already repaired, old snapshots (`summary_json`), old hint/verdict parameter counts, alarms opened before the deploy?
5. **Load fails / partial data.** The UI never derives a success / green / "complete" / "all clear" state from null or partial data. Unknown ≠ OK ≠ missing.
6. **Scoped viewer.** What does a scoped admin, AUDIT, USER, TEAM_ADMIN and 7/24 operator see? Lists are scope-filtered (e.g. `/admin/users`), so an id missing from a scoped list is UNKNOWN, not "absent". A button shows only when the write endpoint would accept it (`can_edit` = permission AND scope).
7. **Time.** Europe/Istanbul day and month boundaries, the current unfinished month, handovers (on-call), and "now" vs "at the time it happened" (who received the opening, which rule was in force for a stored month).
8. **Notifications.** No duplicate (same person via two paths, manual resend), none lost (recovery after an outage), passive users dropped on every channel, recipient language, SKIPPED is not FAILED, every decision leaves a `notification_logs` row, and logged values fit their columns (`recipient_email` / `email_status` are VARCHAR(255) — clip).
9. **Concurrent edits.** Edit forms send only changed fields (never overwrite a concurrent status change); unchanged values are not re-validated; an empty change sends no request.
10. **Project rules.** Names and long text never clipped (wrapping pills need `rounded-md overflow-visible`), 390 / 768 px layouts, opaque user ids, TR + EN keys. Add EN text with an apostrophe through the Edit tool or a Write-tool script, never a heredoc, then check `node -e "import('./src/i18n/en.js')"`.
11. **Gates that read source.** No `);` inside comments in `PermissionCatalog` lists; the audit field is named `auditService`; busy flags cleared in `finally`; `docs/RETENTION_POLITIKASI.md` regenerated after a catalog change; a whitepaper source change (`frontend/src/assets/whitepaper*.md`) needs BOTH `npm run gen:guide-pdf` (PDF + manifest) and, from the repo root, `node scripts/sync-whitepaper.mjs` (root `WHITEPAPER*.md` copies — `whitepaper-sync.test.jsx`); Jacoco class floors (measure with `jacoco:report` when touching a floored class).

## When adding tests

- **Backend**: mirror the package layout under `src/test/java/`. Prefer `@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)` for unit isolation; reach for `@SpringBootTest` only when you need full context (currently exactly one: `SqlSamplesIntegrationTest`). H2 is on the test classpath.
- **Frontend**: place `.test.jsx` next to the component file under `src/test/`. Use `test-utils.jsx#render()`. Add new i18n keys to **both** `TR` and `EN` in the same change or the parity test will fail.
- **Live e2e (2026-10-07):** `frontend/e2e/*.spec.js` mock every `/api` route, so they cannot see frontend↔backend contract bugs (the 20.110 renew-wizard reset was only caught live). `frontend/playwright.live.config.js` + `e2e/live/` drive the REAL UI against the local backend on :8080 (login → manual-certificate journey with cleanup, check-history failure panels, diagnose dialogs). Opt-in only (`E2E_LIVE=1`; CI skips): `bash e2e/live/make-cert-fixtures.sh "$DIR"`, export `E2E_USER` / `E2E_PASS` from `.env` without printing them, then `E2E_LIVE=1 E2E_CERT_DIR="$DIR" npx playwright test -c playwright.live.config.js`. It logs the user's other session out (single-session rule), never triggers notifications, purges every `e2e-live-*` record, and waits if the hourly sweep is < 12 min away. Run it after features that span both sides.

## CI gates (`.github/workflows/`)

- `ci.yml` — Java 25 + Node 24. Backend runs `mvn -B clean verify` (uploads Surefire + Jacoco artifacts). Frontend runs `npm ci` → `lint --if-present` → `build` → `npm audit --audit-level=high --omit=dev` (non-blocking). Helm-lint runs against all three env values files.
  - **Sharded since 2026-10-08.** The private repo's standard runner has 2 vCPU, so vitest (CPU − 1) and Playwright (CPU / 2) each ran ONE worker and CI took 29–50 min.
    - `frontend-unit` runs the vitest suite in 4 shards (`--shard=i/4 --maxWorkers=2 --reporter=blob --coverage`).
    - `VITEST_COVERAGE_SHARD=1` turns off thresholds and reports inside a shard (`vite.config.js`).
    - `frontend-coverage` downloads the blobs, runs `vitest --merge-reports --coverage` (the global threshold gate), then `coverage:floor`.
    - `frontend-e2e` runs Playwright in 4 shards.
    - Locally `npm run test:coverage` is unchanged.
- `docker-build.yml` — multi-arch image build + Trivy scan (HIGH/CRITICAL, currently report-only).
- `release.yml` — runs on `main`; detects bump from conventional commit prefix (`feat:` → minor, `fix:` → patch, `BREAKING CHANGE` → major), writes `VERSION`, publishes Helm chart. Does **not** re-run tests; trusts `ci.yml`.
- `ci.yml` job `backend-postgres-it` (2026-10-02, gating): `postgres:16` service + `mvn -B -Ppostgres-it test` with `IT_DB_URL/USER/PASSWORD` and `IT_DB_REQUIRED=true`. The `@PostgresIntegration` suite (tag `postgres`, excluded from the default surefire run) refuses a non-empty database, boots the context twice and asserts 0 failed schema patches, real indexes, PostgreSQL-only SQL (ON CONFLICT, LATERAL, claims, rollup upserts) and the polled-screen queries. Locally: point `IT_DB_*` at an EMPTY throwaway database (the dev DB user has no CREATEDB).
